- **update-docs**: Sync the docs/ tree to a finished change. Use when a PR/change is ready and its documentation needs updating: reads the diff plus the change's planning artifact (an agent conversation, a superpowers-style spec, or a PLAN.md), then updates only the affected ADRs / design docs / guides / glossary, freezes the planning artifact into docs/history/, and runs the verification gates. Makes NO code changes — it is the doc-sync sibling of code-review (bugs) and design-review (design quality). Args: --base &lt;ref&gt; (default master), --intent &lt;path&gt;, --effort low|medium|high. ([woutervanranst/Arius7](https://github.com/woutervanranst/Arius7/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/update-docs) · ★1)

- **update-log**: 本仓库每轮对话的更新日志流程：一轮对话有文件改动就把它总结成「更新内容」；有【程序】改动时，把改动累积写入根目录「更新日志.md」末尾的「## &lt;下一版本&gt;（预览版）」小节（当前为 0.6 预览版），并把程序版本号升到新预览号（每轮内部位 +1；预览位要动时第三位 +1、内部位归到当前年月号，如 2026-10 → 2611）。版本号是四段 主.次.预览位.内部位，改号时三处一致。用户提出「发布 0.6」时，把该预览小节去掉「（预览版）」转为正式版本小节、程序版本号三处归零为 0.6.0.0 并用 tools\pack-release.ps1 打包。只写功能与行为变化，不写代码/文件/构建/测试等实现细节，技能与仓库约定类改动也不写入。无改动的轮次跳过。与 git-commit-sync 配合：先写日志再一起提交推送。 ([yooxii/ShaoLuAuto](https://github.com/yooxii/ShaoLuAuto/tree/HEAD/.dsh/skills/update-log))
